- 0xquit-operation-26448 (0x32ddb6d6001314de8e2ed23ae5e5c7dcb87a390e): onchain price 0, maxPerWallet 3, hype 43 WARM, official X @jafastar2. STAGED claim ×3 via claim-with-tinfoil.sh for the 12:00 CDT public open (PUBLIC FREE ×3). Tinfoil DANGEROUS -> platform-exception (7/7 OpenSea bundle findings), pre-encode checks green. Fire-time: first attempt aborted start-1s (RPC clock skew, known flaw); attempts 2-3 all reverted in sim with custom error 0xe12d2314(10000,10000) — totalSupply hit maxSupply 10,000: SOLD OUT before the public phase (WL phase at 11:30 CDT finished it; radar had 6928/10000 at 11:15). No claim, no gas spent. ## Update — Wallet Report Card built, verified, and DT.eth engaged (~14:27–14:28 CDT)
- Replaces the earlier plan to build a wallet-rating dashboard and engage @DTmedia_eth with a link. The working artifact is the private fullstack app `wallet-report-card-app` (“Wallet Report Card”); the initial static build `wallet-report-card` failed by design because a live-data app could not ship as a static page.
- The app pulls live ERC-721 history for Base, Ethereum, and Robinhood Chain; computes hold duration, flip rate, time-to-sell, and a transparent 0–100 Diamond-Hands-to-Paper-Hands score; produces an X-ready receipt; and fails closed when data/history is insufficient. It is not publicly shared, and fullstack artifacts have no public link.
- Live smoke tests through the artifact’s `analyzewallet` action succeeded on Foil’s wallet: Base returned Base Blockscout data (1 acquisition cycle/current holding, oldest hold 5.8 days) and Robinhood Chain returned public-RPC data (61 cycles/current holdings, oldest hold 2.9 days). Both correctly returned `insufficient_history` rather than inventing a score.
- @Foil667 replied to DT.eth’s original request with the built result and offered to run their wallet first: https://x.com/Foil667/status/2103929693070352387. The reply text was verified before posting; no other like, repost, follow, or DM occurred.

- Important ABI correction: this deployed registry uses `(address implementation, bytes32 salt, uint256 chainId, address tokenContract, uint256 tokenId)` for `account/createAccount`; salt is second, unlike the initially assumed argument order.
- HoodStreet MCP post-check confirmed `deployed: true`, `codeBytes: 173`, `activationRequired: false`. Current MCP limits remain: no listings/offers from the square and square transfers disabled; signing stays with the square owner.

## Telegram account research completed — decision pending (19:44 CDT handoff)
- Research found no path that is simultaneously free, autonomous, and officially sanctioned. Telegram signup always requires a phone-number-shaped identifier; email-only signup is unavailable.
- Recommended technical path was a one-time SIM-backed OTP number plus a headless MTProto client, estimated ~$0.05–$1, followed by immediate 2FA/username/privacy hardening. This remains unapproved and not started; user was asked whether to authorize the under-$1 spend.
- Durable caveats: Telegram automation via a full user account carries ToS/ban risk, and loss of the persisted session could make an account tied to an expired one-time number unrecoverable. Fragment’s anonymous-number route was reported blocked for this use by KYC/existing-account requirements.
